([tech.hancom.com](https://tech.hancom.com/hwpxformat/))\n\n### 2) The `mimetype` file content is commonly **`application/hwp+zip`** (not `application/haansofthwpx`)\nA Hancom Developer Forum answer explicitly states that for **`.hwpx`**, you can confirm the format via the **top-level `mimetype` file**, and that the stored value is:\n\n- **`application/hwp+zip`** ([forum.developer.hancom.com](https://forum.developer.hancom.com/t/hwp-hwpx-mime-type-whitelist/1641))\n\nA 2026 thread in the `hwp-foss` Google Group also says that Hancom appears to be using **`application/hwp+zip`** and notes that you can see that by opening the file and checking the `mimetype` file. ([goyang.go.kr](https://www.goyang.go.kr/www/publict/ntt/BD_selectPublictNtt.do?publictNttSn=286348&q_publictJobSn=))\n\nThis strongly suggests:\n\n- **`application/haansofthwpx`** is being used by some web servers as a **Content-Type header / download classification**\n- but it is **not the same thing as the internal `mimetype` file content** typically used inside the `.hwpx` ZIP container (which sources above indicate is often `application/hwp+zip`).\n\n### 4) Other MIME types you may see associated with HWP/HWPX\nAn older `hwp-foss` thread about MIME types found in a Linux viewer packaging / shared-mime-info context lists multiple MIME types for `.hwp`, and for `.hwpx` it lists:\n\n- **`application/vnd.hancom.hwpx`** ([groups.google.com](https://groups.google.com/g/hwp-foss/c/64d9HQj3GQA))\n\nThe same thread criticizes the “`application/haansoft...`” style as not ideal practice. ([groups.google.com](https://groups.google.com/g/hwp-foss/c/64d9HQj3GQA))\n\n### 5) “Officially registered” vs “used in practice”\nA Hancom Developer Forum discussion about setting HTTP `Content-Type` for `.hwpx` notes (paraphrased) that **HWPX does not have an officially registered MIME type**, and suggests using **`application/hwp+zip`** only if your client has a custom agreed behavior; otherwise **`application/octet-stream`** can be used since HWPX is ZIP-binary.
